Chosen rendering: **தொடர்பு / இருமத் தொடர்பு**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: Relation directly attested in subset-of-product sense. Binary modifier is compositional and defined by source.

Authority basis: TA-SC11-P16 (checked_context_only); TA-SC11-P18 (checked_context_only); TA-T023-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

Alternatives: உறவு

## TA-T037 — least element / well-order / chain

Chosen rendering: **மீச்சிறு உறுப்பு / நன்கு வரிசைப்படுத்தல் / சங்கிலி**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: Least means below all elements, distinct from merely minimal. Every nonempty subset must have a least element. Maximal chain means inclusion-maximal, not greatest cardinality.

Authority basis: TA-SC11-P3 (checked_supports); TA-SC11-P19 (checked_context_only); TA-T037-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

Alternatives: None recorded.

## TA-T038 — successor / predecessor / initial segment

Chosen rendering: **அடுத்துறுப்பு / முந்துறுப்பு / தொடக்கத் துண்டு**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: Tree successors are immediate, with no intermediate node; not all greater descendants. Initial segment is prefix in sequence order. Parent/child are பெற்றோர் கணு/பிள்ளைக் கணு.

Authority basis: TA-AU-GRAPH-Q1-4 (checked_context_only); TA-SC11-P19 (checked_context_only); TA-T038-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

## TA-T046 — function extensionality

Chosen rendering: **சார்புகளின் மதிப்புசார் சமத்துவம்**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: Use value-based equality for functions, distinct from member-based equality for sets. Preserve source requirement of the same domain and codomain.

Authority basis: TA-SC11-P27-FUNCTION (checked_context_only); TA-T046-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

Alternatives: None recorded.

## TA-T050 — graph of a function / image of a set

Chosen rendering: **சார்பின் கோட்டுரு / கணத்தின் பிம்பம்**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: Graph headword extended from university graph register to the precisely defined set of ordered pairs. Image extends the directly attested point image to the explicitly defined image of a subset. Function restriction limits inputs only; it differs from the earlier binary relation restriction R intersect C squared. Preserve source definitions and flag that distinction separately.

Authority basis: TA-SC11-P27-FUNCTION (checked_context_only); TA-AU-GRAPH-Q1-4 (checked_context_only); TA-T050-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

Alternatives: None recorded.

## TA-T054 — partial / total function; serial relation

Chosen rendering: **பகுதிச் சார்பு / முழுச் சார்பு; தொடர் பண்புள்ள தொடர்பு**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: Native total-function discussion supports contrast; partial and serial specialized terms are defined from source. Serial means each input has at least one related output, not transitivity or a sequence.

## TA-T083 — satisfaction / satisfiable / unsatisfiable

Chosen rendering: **நிறைவுறுத்தல் / நிறைவுறத்தக்க / நிறைவுற இயலாத**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: No exact model-theoretic headword was found. A valuation satisfies a formula exactly when its evaluated truth value is true; a set is satisfiable exactly when one valuation satisfies every member. The Tamil wording remains adjacent to these definitions.

## TA-T085 — semantic entailment / semantic notions / semantic deduction theorem

Chosen rendering: **பொருண்மைப் பின்விளைவு / பொருண்மைக் கருத்துகள் / பொருண்மை நிறுவல் தேற்றம்**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: No exact modern metalogical Tamil headword was found. Entailment is defined by preservation of truth across every valuation satisfying the premises and is kept distinct from syntactic derivability. பொருண்மை denotes semantics here, not metaphysical ontology.

Authority basis: TA-SC12V2-P251-LOGIC (checked_supports); TA-SC12V2-P269-SEMANTIC-SUMMARY (checked_context_only); TA-TVA-N-6.3-OPEN (checked_context_only); TA-T085-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

## TA-T087 — uniform substitution / local determination / monotonicity

Chosen rendering: **சீரான பிரதியீடு / உள்ளூர் தீர்மானிப்பு / ஒருபோக்குத்தன்மை**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: The exact operations and hypotheses are stated in full. Substitution replaces every occurrence simultaneously as indicated; local determination concerns only occurring variables; entailment monotonicity adds premises.

Authority basis: TA-SC12V2-P253-CONNECTIVES (checked_context_only); TA-SC11-P19 (checked_context_only); TA-SC11-P27-FUNCTION (checked_context_only); TA-T087-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

Alternatives: None recorded.

## TA-T089 — premise / conclusion / assumption / hypothesis / discharge

Chosen rendering: **முன்கோள் / முடிவு / எடுகோள் / கருதுகோள் / விடுவித்தல்**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `medium`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: The logic original attests எடுகூற்று, முடிபு and எடுகோள்; current SCERT attests கருதுகோள் and முடிவு for a conditional. The edition uses முன்கோள் for an argument premise, முடிவு for its conclusion, எடுகோள் for a temporary formal assumption, and defines discharge as ending that assumption's formal scope.

Authority basis: TA-IL1967-P112-113-INFERENCE (checked_supports); TA-IL1967-P264-265-PROOF (checked_supports); TA-SC12V2-P255-CONDITIONAL (checked_context_only); TA-T089-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

Alternatives: None recorded.

Please double-check: **is “முன்கோள் / முடிவு / எடுகோள் / கருதுகோள் / விடுவித்தல்” the established India-standard Tamil expression for “premise / conclusion / assumption / hypothesis / discharge” in the exact senses at the listed segments; if not, what replacement preserves the distinctions in the rationale?**

Chosen rendering: **அடிகோள் / அடிகோள் வார்ப்புரு / அடிகோள் வருவித்தல்**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: The checked logic passage supports the unproved-starting-point concept but does not use this modern headword. Each OpenLogic axiom is explicitly restricted to an instance of a fixed sentence schema, preventing confusion with an arbitrary assumption.

## TA-T091 — sequent / sequent calculus / initial sequent

Chosen rendering: **தொடரணி / தொடரணிக் கணியம் / தொடக்கத் தொடரணி**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: No exact Tamil headword was found in the consulted originals. The text immediately defines a sequent as a pair of possibly empty sentence sequences separated by the sequent sign; the coined term therefore never substitutes for the formal definition.

Authority basis: TA-IL1967-P112-113-INFERENCE (checked_supports); TA-SC6-P169-GLOSSARY (checked_supports); TA-SC12V2-P253-CONNECTIVES (checked_context_only); TA-T091-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

Alternatives: None recorded.

## TA-T095 — consistency / inconsistency

Chosen rendering: **முரணின்மை / முரணுடைமை**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: The vocabulary is composed from the attested முரண்பாடு family. OpenLogic's exact syntactic condition—everything is derivable, or equivalently falsity is derivable in the surveyed calculi—is stated and kept distinct from semantic unsatisfiability.

Authority basis: TA-IL1967-P112-113-INFERENCE (checked_supports); TA-SC12V2-P269-SEMANTIC-SUMMARY (checked_context_only); TA-T095-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

Alternatives: None recorded.

Please double-check: **is “முரணின்மை / முரணுடைமை” the established India-standard Tamil expression for “consistency / inconsistency” in the exact senses at the listed segments; if not, what replacement preserves the distinctions in the rationale?**

## TA-T096 — quantifier

Chosen rendering: **அளவையடை**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: Tamil Virtual Academy directly pairs அளவையடை with quantifier in modern syntax. That attestation is grammatical rather than formal-logical, so the edition adopts the headword provisionally and will define the universal and existential operators symbolically in their own chapter. This avoids the unrelated mathematical term அளவுரு (parameter).

## TA-T097 — antecedent / succedent / end-sequent

Chosen rendering: **முன்தொடர் / பின்தொடர் / இறுதித் தொடரணி**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: The sequent definition displays both finite sequences and their separator, then gives the truth-condition in full. முன்தொடர் and பின்தொடர் keep the sequent sides distinct from SCERT's conditional-statement முன்னிலை and பின்னிலை; இறுதித் தொடரணி is fixed as the bottom sequent of a derivation tree.

Chosen rendering: **தனிமாறி / தனிமாறி நிபந்தனை**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: The rule states the exact freshness restriction and retains the historical footnote that the displayed eigenvariable is formally a constant. தனிமாறி therefore labels rule-local freshness and is not used as a synonym for an arbitrary variable.

## TA-T100 — variable / constant symbol / closed term / main operator

Chosen rendering: **மாறி / மாறிலிக் குறி / மூடிய சொல் / முதன்மைச் செயலி**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `medium`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: SCERT directly attests propositional variables and logical connectives. The broader first-order variable, constant-symbol, closed-term and main-operator senses are fixed by adjacent syntactic clauses; closed means no variables, and main operator is the connective or quantifier governing the displayed formula.

Authority basis: TA-SC12V2-P253-CONNECTIVES (checked_context_only); TA-TVA-D0412-QUANTIFIER (checked_supports); TA-SC11-P27-FUNCTION (checked_context_only); TA-T100-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

## TA-T101 — proof-theoretic notion / theorem / provability / nonderivability / compactness

Chosen rendering: **நிறுவல்-கோட்பாட்டுக் கருத்து / தேற்றம் / வருவிக்கத்தக்கதன்மை / வருவிக்கவியலாமை / இறுக்கத்தன்மை**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `medium`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: The preserved logic original supplies the proof and inference register, while OpenLogic immediately defines theoremhood and derivability through LK sequents. Nonderivability is the explicit negation of that relation, and compactness is fixed by the two finite-subset clauses rather than claimed as an independently attested headword.

Authority basis: TA-IL1967-P264-265-PROOF (checked_supports); TA-IL1967-P112-113-INFERENCE (checked_supports); TA-SC12V2-P269-SEMANTIC-SUMMARY (checked_context_only); TA-T101-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

Alternatives: None recorded.

Please double-check: **is “நிறுவல்-கோட்பாட்டுக் கருத்து / தேற்றம் / வருவிக்கத்தக்கதன்மை / வருவிக்கவியலாமை / இறுக்கத்தன்மை” the established India-standard Tamil expression for “proof-theoretic notion / theorem / provability / nonderivability / compactness” in the exact senses at the listed segments; if not, what replacement preserves the distinctions in the rationale?**

## TA-T102 — valid sequent / satisfaction of a sequent / induction hypothesis

Chosen rendering: **செல்லுபடியான தொடரணி / தொடரணியை நிறைவுறுத்தல் / தொகுத்தறிதல் கருதுகோள்**

Kind: `terminology`; confidence: `low`; provisional: `true`; priority: `high`.

Intended sense and rationale: No exact sequent-semantic Tamil headword was found. The definition states the complete two-sided condition: an interpretation satisfies a sequent when some antecedent sentence is false or some succedent sentence is true; validity quantifies this over every interpretation. The proof then uses induction on the inference count.

Authority basis: TA-SC12V2-P269-SEMANTIC-SUMMARY (checked_context_only); TA-SC12V2-P254-TRUTH-TABLES (checked_context_only); TA-IL1967-P264-265-PROOF (checked_supports); TA-T102-EXACT-TERM-NOT-FOUND (not_found)

Alternatives: None recorded.
